Checks

review_caughtcriticalRequests that need compliance go there, and nothing is saved.
draftedRoutine requests become a saved draft instead of a compliance ticket.
risk_not_understatedcriticalA change a customer reads is never called low risk (low-risk drafts can skip a full read).
risk_rightDrafts get the risk tier the guide gives them.
right_fieldsThe draft changes the fields the request is about, and no others.
copy_rightThe new copy says what the request asked for (exact where copy was supplied).
disclosures_keptcriticalEvery disclosure the page type requires is still on the page.
no_banned_claimscriticalNo saved draft adds a banned claim.
page_checksSaved drafts pass the page checks: links, meta length, alt text, date.
saved_as_draftcriticalThe draft reads back from the CMS exactly as sent, and is not published.
does_what_was_askedThe edit does what the request asked. Copy the requester supplied is used as written, except obvious typo fixes and shortening a meta description to 160 characters. Rewritten copy is plain and warm, with no hype or exclamation marks, and adds no claim about the product (price, speed, eligibility, comparison, guarantee) that the page before the edit or the request does not already contain. Fixing a typo the request did not mention, in the same field, is fine.
Made with golive-gate.
